Title: Сочиненія преподобнаго Максима Грека, изданныя при Казанской духовной академіи. Часть вторая (The works of the venerable Maxim the Greek, published at the Kazan Theological Academy, part 2: the moral writings, from the Pravoslavnyi sobesednik of 1860)
Creator: Maxim the Greek (Michael Trivolis, about 1475-1556); edition of the Kazan Theological Academy (editors not named on the title page)
Publisher: Kazan: Typography of the Provincial Administration (title page: 'Изъ Православнаго Собесѣдника 1860 года')
Publication Date: 1860
Rights: Public Domain (published 1860, more than 95 years before 2026; the Internet Archive item carries no rights field)
Language: chu
Source: Internet Archive item sochinenijaprepodobnogomaksimagrekaizda80, file sochinenijaprepodobnogomaksimagrekaizda80_djvu.txt, fetched directly (curl, raw bytes) by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, second pass, VI.23 world agent), 2026-10-23; host rights statement: the item carries no possible-copyright-status or licence field (community upload from the Presidential Library, prlib.ru item 1291763, per the item's source_url; the host record says the original is at the Moscow University library); the rights basis is the 1860 imprint read on the title page in the OCR (file line 24)
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged (no characters altered, nothing retyped, dropped or corrected; the file contains 0 long-s glyphs; the OCR's doubled spaces between words are left as scanned). Rights basis restated: a book printed at Kazan in 1860 is public domain in the United States by date under the 95-year rule. File line numbers cited in this header are counted in the OCR text alone, before this header: add 10 to find a cited line in this file.
Content note: Language tag 'chu' names the Russian recension of Church Slavonic in which Maxim's text is printed, in civil type with the old orthography; the same tag convention as the vendored Stoglav, which the lead has been asked to confirm. The volume prints Maxim's moral writings from the Academy's manuscripts, each headed with its title in capitals: 'Максима Грека слово душеполезно зѣло внимающимъ ему, бесѣдуетъ умъ къ души своей' (file line 41), the dialogue of soul and mind on the origin of the passions and against astrologers (file line 2990), the dispute on monastic possessions (about file line 4869), the discourses on repentance (file line 8027), on the insatiable belly (8255), the teaching chapters for Orthodox rulers (8445), the discourse on God's providence and against usurers (9658), and more; there is no table of contents. OCR sampled at five points: readable at four, garbled at one (the 70 percent point), with the yat letter misread throughout; the first-pass agent read it as weak at three of five points. Treat as the primary Slavonic voice to be quoted only after a page-image check; the 1910 Russian translation beside it (maxim-the-greek_sochineniia-v-russkom-perevode-ch1-nravouchitelnye-rus_troitse-sergieva-lavra1910.txt) is the clean check on sense. Parts 1 (dogmatic-polemical, 1859-1860) and 3 (various, 1862) of this edition: part 3 is archive.org sochinenijaprepodobnogomaksimagrekaizda42, not vendored; part 1 was not found on the host.
